# alephone#11 -- deny-list for -Wl,-unexported_symbols_list, ppc slice only.
# Hides libstdc++.a/__gnu_cxx/__cxxabiv1 symbols from this binary's exported
# symbol table so dyld's weak-symbol coalescing can't rebind Leopard's
# system libstdc++.6.dylib's internal calls into our statically-linked copy
# (two ABI-incompatible libstdc++ runtimes sharing code = indirect call into
# garbage). Everything else, including all of libgcc.a, stays exported --
# an earlier ALLOW-list-of-just-_main attempt hid too much and crashed
# Tiger's older dyld at startup. See scripts/build.sh's COMMON_LDFLAGS
# comment for the full story.
#
# Generated with the toolchain's own nm against a baseline ppc build (this
# repo's HEAD at generation time, no export-restriction flags applied):
#
#   NM=~/gcc14-ppc/bin/powerpc-apple-darwin8-nm
#   "$NM" -m <baseline-ppc-binary> | awk '{n=$NF}
#     n ~ /^__ZNSt/ || n ~ /^__ZSt/ || n ~ /^__ZN9__gnu_cxx/ || n ~ /^__ZTVSt/ || n ~ /^__ZTISt/ ||
#     n ~ /^__ZNKSt/ || n ~ /^__ZTTSt/ || n ~ /^__ZN10__cxxabiv1/ || n ~ /^__ZNK10__cxxabiv1/ ||
#     n ~ /^__ZTI10__cxxabiv1/ ||
#     n ~ /^__ZTVN9__gnu_cxx/ || n ~ /^__ZTIN9__gnu_cxx/ ||
#     n ~ /^__Znwm/ || n ~ /^__Znam/ || n ~ /^__Zdlm/ || n ~ /^__ZdlPv/ || n ~ /^__ZdaPv/ || n ~ /^__Zdam/ ||
#     n ~ /^__ZnwmRKSt/ || n ~ /^__ZnamRKSt/ || n ~ /^__ZdlPvRKSt/ || n ~ /^__ZdaPvRKSt/ ||
#     n ~ /^__ZT[VITT][SsIiOoDd]$/ || n ~ /^__ZT[VITT][SsIiOoDd][A-Za-z0-9]/ \
#     {print n}' | sort -u
#
# Uses $NF (last field), not a fixed column -- nm -m's linkage-annotation
# field shifts by one word between "external" and "weak external". Verify
# any regeneration with a real DYLD_PRINT_BINDINGS trace on real Leopard
# hardware afterward (0 cross-image libstdc++.6.dylib lazy binds is the
# target) and a real launch on real Tiger hardware (no crash) -- don't trust
# a first pass clean; this list took several rounds to reach zero.
